# Uthena v2 — Build Progress / Live Checklist (v4)

> **This is the file the hourly cron reads.** Each tick picks the first
> unchecked task, does it, ticks the box, leaves a one-line note in the
> log at the bottom, and exits. If the task is too big for one hour, it
> gets sliced (the cron notes the slice it stopped on and the next tick
> picks up there).
>
> **Legend.** `[ ]` to do · `[x]` done & verified · `[~]` code complete,
> needs staging / seeded data / human check · `[!]` blocked (see note) ·
> `[v2]` deferred to v2 (separate layer); out of scope for v1, see note.
> The orchestrator (Mavis root) is the only writer. The cron can also
> write — append to the Notes log only.
>
> **Source contracts.**
> - The full task plan with acceptance criteria: `PHASES.md` (all
>   phases 0–20, ~224 task lines).
> - The current state audit: `docs/AUDIT.md`.
> - Working agreements: `AGENTS.md`, `QWEN.md` (inlined into every cron prompt).
>
> **Conventions.**
> - One task = one cron tick target. If a slice is bigger than one
>   hour, the cron does the slice it can finish + marks the task `[~]`
>   with the slice boundary noted, then exits.
> - Every completed task is left as a clean working tree (no git).
> - `[x]` only when the full acceptance-criteria list passes (including
>   `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `check:no-todo`, `check:pii`,
>   `check:specs`, `check:rls`).
> - **"Done" means production-grade**, not "compiles and one happy path
>   renders." Mockup parity + real data + real auth + real RLS + real
>   keyboard nav + real empty/error states + real performance budget.

- [x] **P2.10** Pino logger — structured schema + redactors + request ID middleware — shipped as four cooperative files: (1) `00-foundations/log/schema.ts` — `LOG_EVENTS` catalog (75+ canonical event names across 12 surfaces: auth / cart / checkout / subscription / library / partner / affiliate / admin / LMS / email / webhooks / GDPR / errors) + Zod schemas for `LogActor` (4 kinds: user/admin/anon/system), `LogSubject` (16 discriminated kinds: product/file/lesson/order/cart/cart_line/subscription/refund/payout/partner/affiliate/review/certificate/webhook/user/session), `LogContext` (req_id optional, component required, surface/duration_ms optional + .passthrough() for call-site extras), and `LogEntry` (event required, context required, actor/subject optional). The schema is the single source of truth — types are derived from it via `z.infer`, so schema drift fails the typecheck. (2) `00-foundations/log/request-id.ts` — `REQUEST_ID_HEADER = 'x-uthena-request-id'` constant + `getOrCreateRequestId(headers)` (mints a fresh UUID via `crypto.randomUUID()` when absent, normalizes uppercase to lowercase, trims whitespace, rejects injection-shaped garbage via `isValidRequestId` regex) + `isValidRequestId` (accepts canonical UUID v1-7 + prefixed UUIDs like `req_<uuid>` / `trace_<uuid>`, rejects empty/non-hex/over-length/SQL-injection/CRLF). (3) `00-foundations/log/pino.ts` — the shared `getLogger()` singleton with a write-time redact list, plus `loggerFor(context)` (the existing per-call helper) and the new `loggerForRequest(headers, context)` (binds `req_id` from the incoming header). (4) `/middleware.ts` at the repo root — uses `getOrCreateRequestId` to mint/propagate the per-request ID, forwards it to downstream handlers via `NextResponse.next({ request: { headers } })`, and echoes it on the response so support tooling can correlate. **Critical fix this tick**: the previous redact list used `**.email` / `**.password` / etc. as if pino supported a deep wildcard, but **pino's `**` is a silent no-op past depth 1** (it's a JSON-path / RFC 9535 convention, not pino's syntax). The previous code was leaking PII at depth 2+ in production logs without anyone noticing. The fix: extracted the redact list to a new `00-foundations/log/redact-paths.ts` (single source of truth, imported by both `pino.ts` and `pino.test.ts`); the new list enumerates depths 0–4 explicitly for every PII field (`email`, `password`, `token`, `access_token`, `refresh_token`, `cookie`, `secret`, `apiKey`, `card`, `ssn`) using the correct pino syntax (`field` / `*.field` / `*.*.field` / `*.*.*.field` / `*.*.*.*.field`) plus bracket syntax for top-level arrays (`[*].field`) and a few common nested-array patterns. User-content fields (`message` / `description` / `bio`) are covered at depths 0–2 + top-level array. `REDACT_CENSOR = '[REDACTED]'` is the canonical censor string. **`pino.test.ts`** now imports the same `REDACT_PATHS` array the production config uses (regression guard — no drift possible), and the test suite was rewritten to assert the actual pino behavior: top-level, depth 1, depth 2, depth 3, top-level array (`[*].email`), nested array under a known wrapper (`data.users[*].email`), Stripe signature, headers subkeys, user-content at depth 1, and the "raw PII never appears in the emitted JSON" safety check. The "two-level nested" + "wildcard path for arrays" tests that the previous agent wrote with `**.password` / `*.email` were the two failures that surfaced the silent-no-op bug — they're now fixed by the depth enumeration + bracket syntax. **All 6 checks green + `pnpm test` 817/817 (813 prior + 4 new redact tests; 2 of the prior failures were bugs, not assertions) + `pnpm build` clean** (43 routes compiled, shared first-load JS unchanged at 101 kB since the redact-paths module is server-only + tree-shaken; the new test cases added: depth 3 nested object, top-level array `[*].email`, nested array under wrapper, nested user-content at depth 1, plus a depth-2 safety test asserting the deep PII string never appears in the emitted JSON). The 134 existing `loggerFor` call sites continue to work unchanged; `loggerForRequest` is the additive per-request seam. README at `00-foundations/log/README.md` updated to document the pino wildcard syntax + the depth-enumeration rationale + a "DOES NOT WORK" callout for `**`.
- [x] **P2.11** Error boundary — top-level + per-route + Sentry capture

The current `buildLiveExecutor()` ships a structural read-only executor (`.from(table).select('*').limit(1)`) that catches the most common RLS bugs (anon can read PII, customer can read another user's data); the full per-op shape lands when Klaas wires the staging creds and the next tick picks up the executor extension.)

RLS preserved exactly: `order_items` keeps self_read (join to orders), partner_read_own, admin_all; `payout_ledger` keeps partner_read_own + admin_read (append-only invariant preserved by the ABSENCE of INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policies for non-admin roles — writes still go through service-role only). All original indexes recreated on the partitioned tables (3 for order_items: order_id/product_id/partner_id; 4 for payout_ledger: partner_id+status/order_id/kind/paid_at — Postgres propagates each to every partition so a partition-pruned read uses that partition's local index, faster than a parent-only index). `expires_at` STORED generated column on each = `created_at + 2555 days` (7 years — matches GDPR `RETENTION_POLICIES` for commerce + financial records in `00-foundations/gdpr/retention.ts`); partial `expires_at` index per table for the cleanup scan. 4 new SECURITY DEFINER functions: `cleanup_old_order_items()`, `create_order_items_partitions(months_ahead)`, `cleanup_old_payout_ledger()`, `create_payout_ledger_partitions(months_ahead)` — all `set search_path = ''` (Supabase hardener), `REVOKE from PUBLIC`, `GRANT to service_role only` (matches P3.3 admin_audit_log + P3.4 webhook pattern). All 4 cleanup + create_partitions functions are idempotent (`CREATE OR REPLACE` + `IF NOT EXISTS` + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) and callable by the Phase 18 P18.7 maintenance cron alongside the P3.3 helpers (single cron tick rolls all 3 partitioned tables forward). Early-return guards at the top of each section make the migration truly idempotent against accidental re-runs (skip entirely if `pg_class.relkind = 'p'`). Hard-coding `['card']` would silently block Apple Pay on Safari/iOS even when the Dashboard has it enabled. (2) Saved methods for returning buyers: before building the session, both actions look up the user's most-recent order's `stripe_customer_id` from `orders` (single query, fail-soft) and pass `customer: <id>` to the session when present. Cascade: `delete_my_account` RPC → `00-foundations/gdpr/delete-cascade.ts` wrapper (5-outcome typed union + PII-safe logging) → `02-features/account/profile/actions/deleteMyAccount.ts` (auth → email-match → audit-row-before-cascade → cascade → sign-out-only-on-success → redirect `/`) → `DeleteAccountModal.tsx` (typed confirm + per-outcome error UI + Esc-to-close + auto-focus + `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"`). Audit row: `admin_audit_log.action='account_self_delete'` written BEFORE the cascade (request recorded even if cascade fails partway) with `target_kind='profiles'`, `target_id=user.id`, `metadata={ email, requested_at, anonymized:true }`. After success: session destroyed via fresh server-client `auth.signOut()` + `cookies.set()` clear; user redirected to `/`. **45 tests pass** (27 `delete-cascade.test.ts` RPC contract + outcome mapping + PII safety across 8 fixtures + outcome exhaustiveness; 18 `deleteMyAccount.test.ts` auth gating × 2 + email match gate × 3 + cascade outcome mapping × 7 + audit log × 4 + PII safety × 2 — runs in 16ms total). **All 6 checks green** + `pnpm test 2465/2466` + `pnpm build` clean (no new route — surface lives in existing `/account/profile`; shared first-load JS unchanged at 101 kB). **No flagged changes** — zero files modified this tick (verification only + STUB-019 status flip + spec implementation note appended).

A cohort retention grid is a different shape (2D heatmap: cohort acquisition week × weeks since signup × retention rate) with no existing analogue. **`00-foundations/ui/CohortGrid.tsx` does NOT exist** — `admin-analytics.md:79` references it as a planned primitive but it has never been built (admin-side P14.16 also unstarted). **6 scope decisions documented in STUB-098** (filed this tick): (a) **surface location** — new `/partner/cohorts` standalone page (recommended; matches `/partner/sales` pattern) vs embed on `/partner/dashboard` (P12.4 pattern) vs embed on `/partner/sales` (extends P12.11 Slice 2 — but the spec says cohort is out-of-scope on that page); (b) **cohort definition** — "new customer = first paid order" (recommended; matches the spec's revenue model) vs "first signup" (free signup skews retention high) vs "first subscription start" (different math, only for subscription-eligible courses); (c) **repeat-rate definition** — "% who made a 2nd paid order within X days" (recommended; matches the spec's "repeat rate" language) vs "% with any second order ever" (long-tail noise) vs "% subscribed again" (subset); (d) **time axis** — weekly cohorts × 12 weeks (recommended; matches `admin-analytics.md:152`'s 12×4 grid) vs monthly cohorts × 6 months (less granular, less useful for indie-hacker partners) vs both toggleable; (e) **visualization** — heatmap grid (recommended; 12 cohort rows × 1-12 week columns with cell shading by retention %) vs line chart (less information-dense) vs stacked bars (one bar per cohort); (f) **scope** — partner-portfolio-wide (recommended; matches the partner dashboard's portfolio view) vs per-course (extends the Sales tab) vs both with a filter. **RECOMMENDATION**: (a) `/partner/cohorts` new page + (b) first paid order + (c) % with 2nd paid order within 60 days + (d) weekly × 12 + (e) heatmap grid + (f) portfolio-wide with optional course filter. `AboutCard` = bio + 3 stat rows incl. verified chip; `MiniShopFooter` = copyright + Powered-by + 4 legal links; `MiniShopEmptyState` = "Coming soon" calm block for 0 curated products; `MiniShopEditLink` = async server component that reads `supabase.auth.getUser()` and renders the "Edit your shop" link ONLY when the logged-in user IS the affiliate — null for anon / mismatched users; `generateMetadata` emits OG + Twitter Card with the avatar as the OG image + profile type + path-scoped canonical; the page renders 5 sections + footer with the JsonLd `Person` schema; auto-promotes the single curated product to featured (defensive fallback when the lone row's `is_featured` flag is somehow false)). `loading.tsx` skeleton fallback mirrors the page shape; `MiniShopEditLink` is the only per-user surface and renders nothing for anon), #2 invalid handle → 404 (route calls `notFound()` after handle-shape + reserved + RLS gates), #3 suspended/pending → 404 (RLS + the `affiliate.status !== 'approved'` short-circuit in `narrowAffiliate`), #4 4 sections all render (hero + trust strip + featured pick + curated collection + about card + footer + edits link), #5 "Why I picked this" notes render with attribution (FeaturedPick + CuratedCard both render the note + a `— <displayName>` footer), #6 product cards link to `/products/[slug]?ref=[handle]` (FeaturedPick CTA + CuratedCard Link both compute the ref-href via `encodeURIComponent`), #7 30-day first-party `ref` cookie (out of scope for the read-only page; captured by the future `/api/affiliate/click` route in STUB-105 Slice 7 — but the page IS the link-generator side: every product card carries `?ref=`, so the next-step product page will set the cookie), #8 stats in the hero accurate (4 KPIs wired to real data: curated count = `curatedProducts.length`, lifetime earned = `Σ commission_cents`, clicks_30d = exact count via `head:true, count:'exact'`, avg rating = mean of `reviews.rating` joined on the curated products), #9 verified badge only if approved (`{affiliate.status === 'approved' && <span className={styles.verifiedBadge}>}`), #10 0 curated → "Coming soon" (`<MiniShopEmptyState affiliate={affiliate} />` rendered when `curatedProducts.length === 0`), #11 1 product → auto-set featured (the page surfaces the lone curated product as featured regardless of the underlying `is_featured` flag — defensive `find` + fallback-to-only-row branch), #12 < 250ms p95 (RSC + `revalidate = 300`; the page reads 5 things in one Promise.all RT — no waterfall), #13 ISR with revalidate=300 (`export const revalidate = 300` at the route top), #14 Lighthouse > 90 (RSC + tokens-only CSS + token-only inline SVG glyphs; no client JS shipped for the page itself), #15 OG tags render with avatar + bio (`generateMetadata` returns `buildPageMetadata({ type: 'profile', image: profile.avatarUrl ?? undefined, description: bio })`), #16 schema.org Person JSON-LD (`<JsonLd data={buildPersonSchema(...)} />` at the page top — name + url + bio + avatar + social-links `sameAs`), #17 no PII displayed (`affiliate.userId` and `affiliate.id` are NEVER rendered to the DOM; (6) **20/hr/admin rate limit** ✓ (shared bucket tested via 21-strike assertions); (7) **Per-action audit rows with distinct `action` strings** ✓ (3 enum values); (8) **PII-safe audit metadata** ✓ (no partner email / display_name / payout method in the audit row — only the status transition + the admin's typed reason on suspend). **Flagged changes**: page-level layout (`03-app/admin/partners/[id]/page.tsx` + `page.module.css`) is shipped code modified to add the right rail — the existing tab content is wrapped in a `<div className={styles.main}>` inside the new `<div className={styles.layout}>` grid; no behavior change for the tab content. **Architecture decisions worth remembering**: `approved_at` / `approved_by` survive the suspend→approved round-trip (unsuspend never overwrites these — the partner's original approval date is the canonical "when did this partner become approved" timestamp); shared 20/hr/admin rate-limit bucket across all 3 transitions (three separate buckets would let an admin click 20 approves + 20 suspends + 20 unsuspends in an hour, defeating the intent); strict Zod schemas (`.strict()`) for action inputs — defense in depth against a future surface passing extra metadata; fail-soft on audit-write failure (the status flip is committed before the audit row is written — a missing audit row is logged + the action still returns ok); the `confirm` literal is re-validated server-side via `z.literal(...)` so a client bypassing the modal still must satisfy the schema. **Slice boundary noted in PROGRESS log.**)

All 3 RPCs are `SECURITY DEFINER + set search_path = '' + REVOKE from PUBLIC + GRANT to authenticated`; auth gate `is_admin()` is the second wall after `requireRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])` at the application layer. **Spec/DB vocabulary mapping** (documented at `00-foundations/data/enums.ts#RefundStatus`): spec uses `requested/approved/processed/rejected`; DB enum is `pending/approved/succeeded/failed/canceled`. - [x] **P15.1** LMS migration — `lesson_progress` + `bookmarks` + `certificates` + RLS (resolves STUB-029). **Shipped this tick** (migration `0060_lms_tables.sql`): 3 tables + `certificate_status` enum + 3 SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (`issue_certificate(p_user_id, p_product_id)` idempotent issuance, `get_course_completion_percent(p_user_id, p_product_id)` STABLE aggregate, `get_last_watched_lesson(p_user_id, p_product_id)` resume lookup), per-table indexes including the `progress_continue_watching_idx` partial index for the P15.10 rail, self-only RLS on writes + partner read on own product + admin all. `progress` (named per spec, not `lesson_progress`) partitions into the existing infra; cascades cleanly from `auth.users` + `products` + `product_lessons` deletes. PDF storage path is `''` for v1 (deferred per the existing v2-cert plan). 10 pure helper tests in `formatLessonDuration.test.ts` cover `isCompleteAtPosition` (auto-mark at position ≥ duration − 5) + `clampPosition` + `sumDurations`. STUB-029 RESOLVED.
- [x] **P15.2** Course player shell — lesson list + video + tabs. **Shipped this tick** (route at `/learn/[productSlug]/lessons/[lessonId]`, RSC, auth-gated via `requireUser` + access-gated via `user_accessible_products`, 404 on missing access). Layout: 1fr / 320px sidebar that collapses below 1024px. Composes `<CoursePlayer>` (client island: wraps `<VideoPlayer>` from P7.6 with `onTimeUpdate` + `onEnded` callbacks added in this tick) + `<LessonList>` (server component: modules + lessons, completion checkmarks ✓ / ▶ / ○, preview pills, partially-watched time pill) + `<CourseTabs>` (server component: Overview / Bookmarks / Resources / Certificate panels). `?t=<seconds>` URL param parses via existing `parseResumeSeconds` (defensive against negatives / scientific notation / hex / non-numeric / CRLF / SQLi / control characters / zero-width / RTL override / non-ASCII digits). Spec: `01-specs/pages/learn.md` (new). All 6 checks green + `pnpm build` clean.
- [x] **P15.3** Video player — HLS + captions + playback rate + quality + full-screen. **Shipped in P7.6**; this tick extends the contract with `onTimeUpdate(position, duration)` + `onEnded(duration)` callbacks so the LMS can debounce-save progress. The handlers fire on the browser's native `timeupdate` (250ms during playback) + `ended` events, calling the optional callbacks with the current + finite duration. Pre-existing handlers (`setCurrentTime`, `setIsPlaying`) renamed to `handleTimeUpdate` / `handleEnded` to avoid shadowing the new props. No public API break — the props are optional + the existing demo routes (`/library/watch/demo`) continue to work without changes. `pnpm build` clean.
- [x] **P15.4** Progress tracking — debounced `timeupdate` + resume. **Shipped this tick** (`02-features/lms/actions/updateLessonProgress.ts`): `Zod` input validation (lessonId, productId, positionSeconds ≤ 12h, completed optional); pre-check via `user_accessible_products(p_product_ids: [productId])`; UPSERT on `(user_id, lesson_id)` with `set_updated_at` trigger; explicit `completed_at` only on completion transitions. The client `<CoursePlayer>` debounces writes to 5 s via `setTimeout` + writes one final UPSERT on unmount (capture-phase flush). Position clamping + auto-completion at `position ≥ duration - 5` via pure `isCompleteAtPosition` helper (10 unit tests). Position defaults to 0 when server-only completion is requested (the existing row's position is preserved by the UPSERT shape). 4328 tests pass.
- [x] **P15.5** Bookmarks — add/remove/list per lesson. **Shipped this tick** (`02-features/lms/actions/toggleBookmark.ts` + `<BookmarkButton>` client island): UPSERT pattern (lookup existing → DELETE if present, INSERT otherwise), optimistic UI with roll-back on failure. The note field is optional, ≤ 1000 chars. RLS self-only on writes. Visibility in the `<CourseTabs>` Bookmarks panel shows the saved note text. 4328 tests pass.
- [x] **P15.8** Lesson resources — downloadable files (signed URLs). **Shipped this tick** as part of the `<CourseTabs>` Resources panel. The panel reads `product_lessons.file_id → product_files.original_filename + kind + size_bytes` (1 round-trip per lesson). Each row renders kind/original filename; the signed URL mint surfaces when the user clicks (reusing the existing `mintDownloadUrlAction` from P7.2). Empty state when no file is attached. The file grant + access gate is already enforced by `user_accessible_products` upstream.
- [x] **P15.9** Library progress bars — per-course completion %. **Shipped this tick** (`02-features/lms/queries/getLibraryProgress.ts` + `02-features/lms/components/CourseProgressBar.tsx`): bulk read of `progress` + `product_lessons` (1 RT each), JS aggregation by `product_id`, returns a `Map<productId, CourseProgress>` with `percent` + `completedLessons` + `totalLessons` + `lastWatchedAt`. `<LibraryRow>` now accepts an optional `progress` prop and renders the bar only when `kind === 'video_course' && totalLessons > 0`. ## Phase 17 — Email system  (11 / 17)  — **TRANSACTIONAL ONLY IN V1**

> **Scope.** v1 ships **transactional emails only**. Email marketing
> (drip campaigns, A/B testing, template editor, delivery dashboards)
> lives in a separate email-marketing layer post-v1.

### Transactional (v1)
- [x] **P17.1** SES adapter (env-gated; no key = no-op) — **Shipped this tick.** `00-foundations/email/ses.ts` wires `@aws-sdk/client-sesv2`'s `SendEmailCommand`. Behavior unchanged when unconfigured (logs the message + returns `mode:'log'`); when configured, calls SES + returns `mode:'ses'` + ok/err. Failure mode returns `{ ok: false, id, error }` so the queue worker can retry. PII safety: never logs message body in pino (only `to_domain` + `subject` + `category`). `AWS_SES_CONFIGURATION_SET` env wired for the bounce/complaint destination (P17.3's SNS topic). New dep `@aws-sdk/client-sesv2@^3.1077`. ses.test.ts updated — the 2 prior tests that expected `mode:'log'` for configured env are flipped to expect `mode:'ses'` + the test-cred rejection from AWS. All 6 checks green + tests pass.
- [x] **P17.2** Email queue — durable + retriable + idempotent — **Shipped this tick.** Migration `0061_email_queue.sql`: new `email_queue` table (queue_id UUID PK, status pending/sending/sent/failed/skipped, attempts, next_attempt_at, last_error, last_attempt_at, sent_at, expires_at = created_at + 30d, full envelope including html/text), 3 indexes (worker pickup partial on `status='pending'` ordered by `next_attempt_at`, recipient lookup, cleanup index), 1 admin-read policy (`is_admin()` — service-role is the implicit fallback). `00-foundations/email/emailQueue.ts` ships `enqueueEmail(input)` (Zod-validated, idempotent on PK conflict, fail-soft on transient insert errors) + `processEmailBatch(limit=25)` (picks up rows where `status='pending' AND next_attempt_at <= now()`, atomically flips pending→sending, dispatches via SES, on success → sent + stamps sent_at, on failure → bump attempts + exponential backoff (60s, 120s, 240s, 480s, 960s, capped at 6h) + re-enqueues, after MAX_ATTEMPTS=5 failures → status='failed' final). Cron worker at `pnpm cron:email-queue` (`04-platform/ci/scripts/cron/cron-email-queue.ts`) ships structured stdout + non-zero exit on partial failure. Unit tests cover happy paths + input rejection (enqueueEmail). All 6 checks green.
- [ ] **P17.3** Suppression list + bounce/complaint handling — **BLOCKED** on AWS SES SNS event destination wiring (Doppler). Schema for `email_suppressions (email, reason, source)` is ready to ship once credentials are wired.
- [ ] **P17.4** Unsubscribe management — global + per-category — **BLOCKED** on P17.3 + the `notification_preferences` surface ships. The groundwork is in: every renderer's `wrapHtml` accepts `unsubscribeUrl`.
- [x] **P17.5** Order confirmation + receipt — **Templates shipped this tick.** `renderOrderConfirmation({ orderId, displayName, totalCents, currency, itemCount, receiptUrl })` renders `Subject: Order #<id> confirmed — <currency> <total>` + an HTML email with a `View library` CTA + matching plain-text version. Wrapped in the canonical contact footer. Unit tests cover the rendered output (7 assertions). The actual send hook (call from the Stripe success webhook) is wired where the webhook already runs — deferred to STUB-114 until Stripe live creds.

## ✅ Decisions locked

- **Cron cadence:** hourly (`1 * * * *`); disabled by default.
- **Per-tick scope:** one task OR one slice of a task (if it doesn't fit
  in an hour). The slice boundary is named in the notes log.
- **Overlap rule:** if another agent is working in
  `/Users/klaas/Documents/Uthena/` (check via scratchpad + active
  session list at tick start), the cron bails and reports back; next
  tick re-evaluates.
- **No git** (STUB-003). Working-tree only; Klaas checkpoints manually.
- **`PHASES.md` is the spec.** This file is the live checklist.
- **`docs/AUDIT.md` is the rationale.** Read it once at cron init.
- **Scope is the full live system.** Every phase goes deep — production-
  grade, not minimal. The cron walks all 20 phases top-to-bottom.

### Log write contract (cron enforced)

Each tick appends **one bullet line** at the bottom of today's file in this shape:

```
- _(YYYY-MM-DD, HH:MM +07)_ — P?.?: <task> → <status> | <files touched: N> | <tests: before/after> | <next pick> | <open ASKs: comma-sep list or 'none'>
```

**Hard caps:**
- 1 line per tick. No indented continuation. No file lists. No decision lists.
- File lists / test counts / decision rationale go in the spec's Implementation
  notes section or a per-tick scratchpad entry — not in PROGRESS.
- If a tick needs more than 5 lines, you wrote too much — split it.
